activations)\\n- Change the observation space (what information the controller sees)\\n- Change the action space (what parameters the controller outputs)\\n- Try different training strategies (reward More exploitation as the budget is consumed\n            pbest_p = max(0.05, 0.2 * (1.0 - progress))\n            \n            prev_gbest_f = gbest_f\n            pop_old = pop.copy()\n            new_pop = np.copy(pop)\n            S_F = []\n            S_CR = []\n            F_i_values = np.zeros(pop_size)\n            CR_i_values = np.zeros(pop_size)\n            \n            sorted_idx = np.argsort(fitness)\n            pbest_num = max(1, int(pop_size * pbest_p))\n            pbest_indices = sorted_idx[:pbest_num]\n            \n            # Generate trial vectors using SHADE current-to-pbest/1\n            for i in range(pop_size):\n                # Sample F_i from Cauchy distribution based on history memory\n                mi = int(rng.randint(memory_size))\n                mu_F = float(memory_F[mi]) * 0.5 + ctrl_F * 0.5\n                mu_CR = float(memory_CR[mi]) * 0.5 + ctrl_CR * 0.5\n                F_i = float(mu_F + 0.1 * rng.standard_cauchy())\n                if F_i <= 0.0:\n                    F_i = 0.1\n                elif F_i > 1.0:\n                    F_i = 1.0\n                CR_i = float(rng.normal(mu_CR, 0.1))\n                CR_i = max(0.0, min(1.0, CR_i))\n                F_i_values[i] = F_i\n                CR_i_values[i] = CR_i\n                \n                # Choose pbest from top pbest_p individuals\n                pbest_i = int(pbest_indices[int(rng.randint(len(pbest_indices)))])\n                pbest_x = pop[pbest_i]\n                \n                # current-to-pbest/1 mutation\n                base = pop[i] + F_i * (pbest_x - pop[i])\n                \n                # r1 from population, r2 from population or archive\n                r1 = int(rng.randint(pop_size))\n                while r1 == i:\n                    r1 = int(rng.randint(pop_size))\n                candidate_r2 = [idx for idx in range(pop_size) if idx != i and idx != r1]\n                if len(archive) > 0:\n                    candidate_r2 += [pop_size + j for j in range(len(archive))]\n                if len(candidate_r2) < 1:\n                    r2 = int(rng.randint(pop_size))\n                    x2 = pop[r2]\n                else:\n                    r2 = int(candidate_r2[int(rng.randint(len(candidate_r2)))])\n                    if r2 >= pop_size:\n                        x2 = archive[r2 - pop_size]\n                    else:\n                        x2 = pop[r2]\n                \n                mutant = base + F_i * (pop[r1] - x2)\n                \n                # Binomial crossover\n                j_rand = int(rng.randint(self.dim))\n                mask = rng.random(self.dim) < CR_i\n                mask[j_rand] = True\n                trial = np.where(mask, mutant, pop[i])\n                trial = np.clip(trial, lb, ub)\n                new_pop[i] = trial\n            \n            # Evaluate trial vectors and apply greedy selection\n            for i in range(pop_size):\n                if evals >= self.max_evals:\n                    done = True\n                    break\n                trial_f = evaluate_problem(problem, new_pop[i])\n                evals += 1\n                \n                if trial_f < fitness[i]:\n                    # Add previous position to archive before replacement\n                    old_x = pop_old[i].reshape(1, -1)\n                    if len(archive) < archive_capacity:\n                        archive = np.vstack([archive, old_x])\n                    else:\n                        replace_idx = int(rng.randint(archive_capacity))\n                        archive[replace_idx] = old_x\n                    \n                    pop[i] = new_pop[i].copy()\n                    fitness[i] = trial_f\n                    S_F.append(F_i_values[i])\n                    S_CR.append(CR_i_values[i])\n                    if trial_f < gbest_f:\n                        gbest_f = trial_f\n                        gbest = new_pop[i].copy()\n            \n            # Update SHADE memory with successful parameters\n            if len(S_F) > 0:\n                memory_F[memory_idx] = float(np.mean(S_F))\n                memory_CR[memory_idx] = float(np.mean(S_CR))\n                memory_idx = (memory_idx + 1) % memory_size\n            \n            # Stagnation detection: restart worst individuals if no improvement for several iterations\n            if gbest_f < prev_gbest_f - 1e-12:\n                stagnation_count = 0\n            else:\n                stagnation_count += 1\n            \n            if stagnation_count >= 5:\n                n_restart = max(1, int(pop_size * 0.15))\n                worst_idx = np.argsort(fitness)[-n_restart:]\n                sigma = (ub - lb) * 0.02 * (1.0 + progress)\n                for idx in worst_idx:\n                    if evals >= self.max_evals:\n                        break\n                    if rng.random() < 0.5:\n                        pop[idx] = np.clip(gbest + sigma * rng.randn(self.dim), lb, ub)\n                    else:\n                        pop[idx] = rng.uniform(lb, ub, self.dim)\"\n    }\n    </json>"

load_opt_vals():\n    17\t    \"\"\"\u52a0\u8f7d dataset.csv \u6587\u4ef6\uff0c\u8fd4\u56de\u4e00\u4e2a\u5b57\u5178 {(function, instance, dimension): opt_val}\"\"\"\n    18\t    dataset_path = os.path.join(os.path.dirname(__file__), 'dataset.csv')\n    19\t    opt_vals = {}\n    20\t    if os.path.exists(dataset_path):\n    21\t        with open(dataset_path, 'r') as f:\n    22\t            reader = csv.DictReader(f)\n    23\t            for row in reader:\n    24\t                key = (int(row['function']), int(row['instance']), int(row['dimension']))\n    25\t                opt_vals[key] = float(row['opt_val'])\n    26\t    return opt_vals\n    27\t\n    28\t\n    29\tdef compute_ri_score(initial_gbest_f, gbest_f, opt_val):\n    30\t    \"\"\"\u8ba1\u7b97 score = (initial_best - best) / (initial_best - opt)\u3002\n    31\t    \n    32\t    \u5176\u4e2d\uff1a\n    33\t    - initial_best = initial_gbest_f\uff08PSO\u521d\u59cb\u5316\u540e\u79cd\u7fa4\u7684\u6700\u4f18\u503c\uff09\n    34\t    - best = gbest_f\uff08PSO\u8fd0\u884c\u7ed3\u675f\u540e\u7684\u6700\u4f18\u503c\uff09\n    35\t    - opt = opt_val\uff08\u7406\u8bba\u6700\u4f18\u503c\uff09\n    36\t    \n    37\t    \u8fd4\u56de\u622a\u65ad\u5230 [0, 1] \u7684 score\u3002\n    38\t    \"\"\"    \n    39\t    if abs(initial_gbest_f - opt_val) < 1e-12:\n    40\t        return 1.0 if gbest_f <= opt_val + 1e-12 else 0.0\n    41\t    \n    42\t    score = (initial_gbest_f - gbest_f) / (initial_gbest_f - opt_val)\n    43\t    return max(0.0, min(1.0, score))\n    44\t\n    45\t\n    46\tdef run_de_and_score(inputs, log_fn=print, mode='test', controller=None, seed=None):\n    47\t    \"\"\"\u8fd0\u884c DQN \u63a7\u5236\u7684 DE \u5e76\u8ba1\u7b97 RI \u8bc4\u5206\u3002\n    48\t    \n    49\t    Args:\n    50\t        inputs: dict from domains.bbob_unconstrained.utils.format_input_dict\n    51\t        Expected keys: 'problem_id','dimension','instance'\n    52\t        log_fn: logging function\n    53\t        mode: 'train' for training DQN (updates model), 'test' for evaluation only\n    54\t        controller: optional external controller (shared across problems)\n    55\t        seed: random seed for DE initialization\n    56\t    \n    57\t    Returns:\n    58\t        prediction (string) in format \"score|gbest_f|initial_gbest_f|opt_val\"\n    59\t    \"\"\"\n    60\t    # lazy import cocoex and DE module from metabbo\n    61\t    import cocoex\n    62\t\n    63\t    this_dir = os.path.join(os.path.dirname(__file__), '..', '..', 'metabbo')\n    64\t    # \u5c06 metabbo \u76ee\u5f55\u6dfb\u52a0\u5230 sys.path\uff0c\u4ee5\u4fbf\u6a21\u5757\u5185\u7684\u76f8\u5bf9\u5bfc\u5165\u53ef\u4ee5\u6b63\u5e38\u5de5\u4f5c\n    65\t    if this_dir not in __import__('sys').path:\n    66\t        __import__('sys').path.insert(0, this_dir)\n    67\t    # load local ec_algorithm module\n    68\t    spec = importlib.util.spec_from_file_location('ec_mod', os.path.join(this_dir, 'ec_algorithm.py'))\n    69\t    ec_mod = importlib.util.module_from_spec(spec)\n    70\t    spec.loader.exec_module(ec_mod)\n    71\t    \n    72\t    # Create or use provided controller\n    73\t    if controller is None:\n    74\t        # load meta learning implementations\n    75\t        spec2 = importlib.util.spec_from_file_location('meta_mod', os.path.join(this_dir, 'meta_learning.py'))\n    76\t        meta_mod = importlib.util.module_from_spec(spec2)\n    77\t        spec2.loader.exec_module(meta_mod)\n    78\t        model_path = os.path.join(this_dir, 'dqn_model.pt')\n    79\t        controller = meta_mod.DiscreteDQNController(model_path=model_path, training=(mode == 'train'), seed=seed)\n    80\t    \n    81\t    # prepare suite filter (dimension + instance)\n    82\t    dim = int(inputs.get('dimension', 10))\n    83\t    inst = int(inputs.get('instance', 1))\n    84\t    max_evals = max(1000, dim * 500)\n    85\t\n    86\t    # \u4ece problem_id \u5b57\u7b26\u4e32\u4e2d\u63d0\u53d6 function \u7f16\u53f7\uff08\u4f8b\u5982 f1_i1 -> 1\uff09\n    87\t    problem_id_str = inputs.get('problem_id', 'f1_i1')\n    88\t    problem_id = int(str(problem_id_str).replace('f', '').split('_')[0])\n    89\t\n    90\t    suite = cocoex.Suite('bbob', '', f'dimensions: {dim} instance_indices:{inst} function_indices: {problem_id}')\n    91\t    # pick the matching problem from the suite\n    92\t    problem = next(iter(suite))\n    93\t\n    94\t    # run DE algorithm using DEOptimizer to get stats\n    95\t    try:\n    96\t        de_opt = ec_mod.DEOptimizer(problem.dimension, getattr(problem, 'lower_bounds', None), getattr(problem, 'upper_bounds', None), max_evals, params={'F': 0.5, 'CR': 0.5}, controller=controller, recorder=None, swarm_size=40)\n    97\t        # \u8bad\u7ec3\u6a21\u5f0f\u4f1a\u66f4\u65b0 DQN \u6a21\u578b\uff0c\u6d4b\u8bd5\u6a21\u5f0f\u53ea\u4f7f\u7528\u6a21\u578b\n    98\t        gbest, stats = de_opt.optimize(problem, train=(mode == 'train'), seed=seed)\n    99\t        gbest_f = stats.get('gbest_f', None)\n   100\t        initial_gbest_f = stats.get('initial_gbest_f', gbest_f)\n   101\t        \n   102\t        # \u8bad\u7ec3\u6a21\u5f0f\u4e0b\u4fdd\u5b58\u6a21\u578b\uff08\u53ea\u5728 controller \u662f\u5185\u90e8\u521b\u5efa\u65f6\u4fdd\u5b58\uff09\n   103\t        if mode == 'train' and controller is not None and hasattr(controller, 'net'):\n   104\t            import torch\n   105\t            model_path = os.path.join(this_dir, 'dqn_model.pt')\n   106\t            torch.save(controller.net.state_dict(), model_path)\n   107\t            log_fn(f\"[TRAIN] Model saved to {model_path}\")\n   108\t    except Exception as e:\n   109\t        return f'error: DE run failed: {e}'\n   110\t\n   111\t    # \u8ba1\u7b97 RI \u5206\u6570\n   112\t    # \u52a0\u8f7d\u7406\u8bba\u6700\u4f18\u503c\n   113\t    opt_vals = load_opt_vals()\n   114\t    opt_val_key = (problem_id, inst, dim)\n   115\t    opt_val = opt_vals.get(opt_val_key, None)\n   116\t    \n   117\t    # \u4f7f\u7528 RI \u8bc4\u5206\u65b9\u5f0f\n   118\t    score = compute_ri_score(initial_gbest_f, gbest_f, opt_val)\n   119\t    \n   120\t    mode_str = \"TRAIN\" if mode == 'train' else \"TEST\"\n   121\t    log_fn(f\"[{mode_str}] f{problem_id} Score: {score}\")\n   122\t    log_fn(f\"[{mode_str}] f{problem_id} gbest_f: {gbest_f}\")\n   123\t    log_fn(f\"[{mode_str}] f{problem_id} initial_gbest_f: {initial_gbest_f}\")\n   124\t    log_fn(f\"[{mode_str}] f{problem_id} opt_val: {opt_val}\")\n   125\t    \n   126\t    # \u8bad\u7ec3\u6a21\u5f0f\u4e0b\u6536\u96c6 loss \u548c return\n   127\t    if mode == 'train':\n   128\t        training_loss = controller.get_total_loss() if hasattr(controller, 'get_total_loss') else None\n   129\t        sweep_return = controller.get_total_reward() if hasattr(controller, 'get_total_reward') else None\n   130\t        if training_loss is not None:\n   131\t            log_fn(f\"[TRAIN] Total DQN loss: {training_loss:.6f}\")\n   132\t        if sweep_return is not None:\n   133\t            log_fn(f\"[TRAIN] Total DQN return: {sweep_return:.4f}\")\n   134\t        return {\n   135\t            'score': score,\n   136\t            'gbest_f': gbest_f,\n   137\t            'initial_gbest_f': initial_gbest_f,\n   138\t            'opt_val': opt_val,\n   139\t            'training_loss': training_loss,\n   140\t            'sweep_return': sweep_return,\n   141\t            'controller': controller\n   142\t        }\n   143\t    \n   144\t    # \u8fd4\u56de\u683c\u5f0f\uff1a\"score|gbest_f|initial_gbest_f|opt_val\" \u4ee5\u4fbf report.py \u89e3\u6790\n   145\t    prediction = f\"{score}|{gbest_f}|{initial_gbest_f}|{opt_val}\"\n   146\t\n   147\t    # \u4e5f\u8fd4\u56de dict \u683c\u5f0f\u4f9b\u6d4b\u8bd5\u811a\u672c\u4f7f\u7528\n   148\t    return {\n   149\t It can load/save a model\n     6\tand be used at inference time inside the DE optimizer.\n     7\t\"\"\"\n     8\tfrom typing import Dict, Any\n     9\timport os\n    10\timport numpy as np\n    11\ttry:\n    12\t    import torch\n    13\t    import torch.nn as nn\n    14\t    import torch.nn.functional as F\n    15\texcept Exception:\n    16\t    torch = None\n    17\t\n    18\tfrom param_controller import ParamController\n    19\t\n    20\timport sys\n    21\tsys.path.insert(0, os.path.dirname(os.path.dirname(os.path.abspath(__file__))))\n    22\tfrom config import SEED, set_global_seed\n    23\t\n    24\tif torch is not None:\n    25\t    class _Net(nn.Module):\n    26\t        def __init__(self, obs_dim, action_dim, seed=None):\n    27\t            super().__init__()\n    28\t            self.fc1 = nn.Linear(obs_dim, 128)\n    29\t            self.fc2 = nn.Linear(128, 128)\n    30\t            self.fc3 = nn.Linear(128, 128)\n    31\t            self.out = nn.Linear(128, action_dim)\n    32\t            \n    33\t            # \u4f7f\u7528Kaiming\u521d\u59cb\u5316\n    43\t            for m in [self.fc1, self.fc2, self.fc3, self.out]:\n    44\t                torch.nn.init.kaiming_uniform_(m.weight, nonlinearity='leaky_relu')\n    45\t                torch.nn.init.zeros_(m.bias)\n    46\t\n    47\t        def forward(self, x):\n    48\t            x = x.type_as(self.fc1.weight)\n    49\t            x = F.leaky_relu(self.fc1(x), 0.2)\n    50\t            x = F.leaky_relu(self.fc2(x), 0.2)\n    51\t            x = F.leaky_relu(self.fc3(x), 0.2)\n    52\t            return self.out(x)\n    53\t\n    54\t\n    55\tclass DQNController(ParamController):\n    56\t    \"\"\"Simple continuous-action DQN controller for DE parameters.\n    57\t\n    58\t    The network outputs unbounded real values; `step` maps them to\n    59\t    DE parameter ranges: F in [0.1, 1.0], CR in [0.0, 1.0].\n    60\t    \"\"\"\n    61\t    def __init__(self, model_path: str = None, obs_dim: int = 3, device: str = 'cpu'):\n    62\t        if torch is None:\n    63\t            raise RuntimeError('torch is required for DQNController')\n    64\t        self.device = torch.device(device)\n    65\t        self.obs_dim = obs_dim\n    66\t        self.action_dim = 2  # normalized progress and relative improvement\n    81\t        progress = min(1.0, evals / max(1.0, max_evals))\n    82\t        if init_f != 0.0 and gbest_f != 0.0 and np.isfinite(init_f) and np.isfinite(gbest_f):\n    83\t            rel_improve = max(0.0, min(1.0, (np.log(abs(init_f)) - np.log(abs(gbest_f))) / max(1.0, abs(np.log(abs(init_f))))))\n    84\t        else:\n    85\t            rel_improve = 0.0\n    86\t        obs = [progress, rel_improve, gbest_f]\n    87\t        \n    88\t        net_dtype = self.net.fc1.weight.dtype\n    89\t        x = torch.tensor(obs, dtype=net_dtype, device=self.device).unsqueeze(0)\n    90\t        \n    91\t        with torch.no_grad():\n    92\t            out_t = self.net(x).squeeze(0).cpu()\n    93\t            try:\n    94\t                out_t = torch.nan_to_num(out_t, nan=0.0, posinf=1e6, neginf=-1e6)\n    95\t            except Exception:\n    96\t                out_np = out_t.numpy()\n    97\t                out_np = np.nan_to_num(out_np, nan=0.0, posinf=1e6, neginf=-1e6)\n    98\t                out_t = torch.from_numpy(out_np)\n    99\t            out_t = torch.clamp(out_t, -10.0, 10.0)\n   100\t\n   101\t        out = out_t.numpy()\n   102\t\n   103\t        # Map F (differential weight) with sigmoid to [0.1, 1.0]\n   104\t        try:\n   105\t            f_raw = float(out[0])\n   106\t            f_sig = 1.0 / (1.0 + float(np.exp(-f_raw)))\n   107\t        except Exception:\n   108\t            f_sig = 0.5\n   109\t        F = 0.1 + (1.0 - 0.1) * f_sig\n   110\t        \n   111\t        # Map CR (crossover probability) with sigmoid to [0.0, 1.0]\n   112\t        try:\n   113\t            cr_raw = float(out[1])\n   114\t            cr_sig = 1.0 / (1.0 + float(np.exp(-cr_raw)))\n   115\t        except Exception:\n   116\t            cr_sig = 0.9\n   117\t        CR = 0.0 + (1.0 - 0.0) * cr_sig\n   118\t        \n   119\t        return {'F': float(F), 'CR': float(CR)}\n   120\t\n   121\t    def save(self, path: str):\n   122\t        torch.save(self.net.state_dict(), path)\n   123\t\n   124\t    def load(self, path: str):\n   125\t        self.net.load_state_dict(torch.load(path, map_location=self.device))\n   126\t\n   127\t\n   128\tclass DiscreteDQNController(ParamController):\n   129\t    \"\"\"Discrete-action DQN controller for DE parameters with online training.\n   130\t\n   131\t    Discretizes F and CR into `bins` equally spaced values and\n   132\t    treats the Cartesian product as the discrete action space.\n   133\t    The network outpu\n   134\t<response clipped>\n   135\trm]\n   136\t        net_dtype = self.net.fc1.weight.dtype\n   137\t        return torch.tensor(obs, dtype=net_dtype, device=self.device)\n   138\t\n   139\t    def step(self, observation: Dict[str, Any]) -> Dict[str, float]:\n   140\t        x = self._obs_to_tensor(observation)\n   141\t\n   142\t        # Epsilon-greedy action selection (only in training mode)\n   143\t        if self.training and self.rng.random() < self.epsilon:\n   144\t            idx = int(self.rng.randint(self.n_actions))\n   145\t        else:\n   146\t            with torch.no_grad():\n   147\t                q = self.net(x.unsqueeze(0)).squeeze(0).cpu().numpy()\n   148\t            idx = int(np.argmax(q))\n   149\t\n   150\t        if self.training:\n   151\t            # Store transition placeholder; reward will be set later via store_reward()\n   152\t            self._last_obs = observation\n   153\t            self._last_action_idx = idx\n   154\t            self._last_obs_tensor = x\n   155\t\n   156\t        return dict(self.actions[idx])\n   157\t\n   158\t    def store_reward(self, reward: float, next_observation: Dict[str, Any], done: bool):\n   159\t        \"\"\"Store a complete transition (obs, action, reward, next_obs, done) in the replay buffer.\"\"\"\n   160\t        # Always track total reward (for both train and test)\n   161\t        self.total_reward += reward\n   162\t        self.current_sweep_reward += reward\n   163\t        \n   164\t        if not self.training:\n   165\t            return\n   166\t\n   167\t        next_obs_tensor = self._obs_to_tensor(next_observation)\n   168\t        transition = (\n   169\t            self._last_obs_tensor.clone(),\n   170\t            self._last_action_idx,\n   171\t            reward,\n   172\t            next_obs_tensor.clone(),\n   173\t            done\n   174\t        )\n   175\t\n   176\t        if len(self.replay_buffer) < self.buffer_capacity:\n   177\t            self.replay_buffer.append(transition)\n   178\t        else:\n   179\t            self.replay_buffer[self.buffer_pos] = transition\n   180\t            self.buffer_pos = (self.buffer_pos + 1) % self.buffer_capacity\n   181\t\n   182\t        self.step_count += 1\n   183\t\n   184\t        # Record return every N updates and reset counter\n   185\t        if self.step_count % self.return_record_interval == 0:\n   186\t            self.reward_history.append(self.current_sweep_reward)\n   187\t            self.current_sweep_reward = 0.0\n   188\t\n   189\t        # Train every update_freq steps\n   190\t        if self.step_count % self.update_freq == 0 and len(self.replay_buffer) >= self.batch_size:\n   191\t            loss = self._train_step()\n   192\t            self.total_loss += loss\n   193\t            self.loss_history.append(loss)\n   194\t\n   195\t    def _train_step(self) -> float:\n   196\t        \"\"\"Perform one DQN training step on a batch from the replay buffer.\"\"\"\n   197\t        batch = self.rng.choice(len(self.replay_buffer), self.batch_size, replace=False).tolist()\n   198\t        batch = [self.replay_buffer[i] for i in batch]\n   199\t\n   200\t        obs_b = torch.stack([t[0] for t in batch])\n   201\t        act_b = torch.tensor([t[1] for t in batch], dtype=torch.long, device=self.device)\n   202\t        rew_b = torch.tensor([t[2] for t in batch], dtype=torch.float32, device=self.device)\n   203\t        next_b = torch.stack([t[3] for t in batch])\n   204\t        done_b = torch.tensor([t[4] for t in batch], dtype=torch.float32, device=self.device)\n   205\t\n   206\t        q_values = self.net(obs_b)\n   207\t        q_sa = q_values.gather(1, act_b.unsqueeze(1)).squeeze(1)\n   208\t\n   209\t        with torch.no_grad():\n   210\t            q_next = self.target_net(next_b)\n   211\t            q_next_max = q_next.max(1)[0]\n   212\t            target = rew_b + self.gamma * (1.0 - done_b) * q_next_max\n   213\t\n   214\t        loss = F.mse_loss(q_sa, target)\n   215\t        self.optimizer.zero_grad()\n   216\t        loss.backward()\n   217\t        torch.nn.utils.clip_grad_norm_(self.net.parameters(), self.max_grad_norm)\n   218\t        self.optimizer.step()\n   219\t\n   220\t        # Update target network periodically\n   221\t        if self.step_count % self.target_update_freq == 0:\n   222\t            self.target_net.load_state_dict(self.net.state_dict())\n   223\t            # Decay epsilon\n   224\t            self.epsilon = max(self.epsilon_end, self.epsilon * self.epsilon_decay)\n   225\t\n   226\t        return loss.item()\n   227\t\n   228\t    def reset_loss(self):\n   229\t        \"\"\"Reset loss and reward counters for a new sweep.\"\"\"\n   230\t        self.total_loss = 0.0\n   231\t        self.total_reward = 0.0\n   232\t        self.current_sweep_reward = 0.0\n   233\t        self.reward_history = []\n   234\t\n   235\t    def get_loss_history(self) -> list:\n   236\t        \"\"\"Return the list of recorded losses.\"\"\"\n   237\t        return self.loss_history\n   238\t\n   239\t    def get_reward_history(self) -> list:\n   240\t        \"\"\"Return the list of recorded returns (every N updates).\"\"\"\n   241\t        return self.reward_history\n   242\t\n   243\t    def get_total_loss(self) -> float:\n   244\t        \"\"\"Return the cumulative training loss.\"\"\"\n   245\t        return self.total_loss\n   246\t\n   247\t    def get_total_reward(self) -> float:\n   248\t        \"\"\"Return the cumulative reward from environment interactions.\"\"\"\n   249\t        return self.total_reward\n   250\t\n   251\t    def action_to_index(self, action: Dict[str, float]) -> int:\n   252\t        a = np.array([action.get('F', 0.8), action.get('CR', 0.9)], dtype=float)\n   253\t        acts = np.array([[aa['F'], aa['CR']] for aa in self.actions], dtype=float)\n   254\t        d = np.sum((acts - a[None, :])**2, axis=1)\n   255\t        return int(np.argmin(d))\n   256\t\n   257\t    def save(self, path: str):\n   258\t        torch.save(self.net.state_dict(), path)\n   259\t\n   260\t    def load(self, path: str):\n   261\t        self.net.load_state_dict(torch.load(path, map_location=self.device))\n\"\n    }\n    </json>",
    "text": "Here are the available tools:\n```\n{'name': 'bash', 'description': 'Run commands in a bash shell\\n* When invoking this tool, the contents of the \"command\" parameter does NOT need to be XML-escaped.\\n* You don\\'t have access to the internet via this tool.\\n* You do have access to a mirror of common linux and python packages via apt and pip.\\n* State is persistent across command calls and discussions with the user.\\n* To inspect a particular line range of a file, e.g. lines 10-25, try \\'sed -n 10,25p /path/to/the/file\\'.\\n* Please avoid commands that may produce a very large amount of output.\\n* Please run long lived commands in the background, e.g. \\'sleep 10 &\\' or start a server in the background.', 'input_schema': {'type': 'object', 'properties': {'command': {'type': 'string', 'description': 'The bash command to run.'}}, 'required': ['command']}}\n\n{'name': 'editor', 'description': 'Custom editing tool for viewing, creating and editing files\\n* State is persistent across command calls and discussions with the user\\n* If `path` is a file, `view` displays the result of applying `cat -n`. If `path` is a directory, `view` lists non-hidden files and directories up to 2 levels deep\\n* The `create` command cannot be used if the specified `path` already exists as a file\\n* If a `command` generates a long output, it will be truncated and marked with `<response clipped>`\\n* The `undo_edit` command will revert the last edit made to the file at `path`\\n\\nNotes for using the `str_replace` command:\\n* The `old_str` parameter should match EXACTLY one or more consecutive lines from the original file. Be mindful of whitespaces!\\n* If the `old_str` parameter is not unique in the file, the replacement will not be performed. Setting `[start_line, -1]` shows all lines from `start_line` to the end of the file.', 'items': {'type': 'integer'}, 'type': 'array'}}, 'required': ['command', 'path']}}\n```\n\nYou can use tools in this format:\n<json>\n{\n    \"tool_name\": ...,\n    \"tool_input\": ...\n}\n</json>\n\nYou can use MULTIPLE tools in a single response by including multiple JSON blocks.\nSTRICTLY FOLLOW THE FORMAT OF TOOL_NAME AND TOOL_INPUT ABOVE.\nDO NOT HALLUCINATE OR MAKE UP ANYTHING.\n\n<json>\n    {\n        \"tool_name\": \"editor\",\n        \"tool_input\": {'command': 'view', 'path': '/hada/metabbo/meta_learning.py'},\n        \"tool_output\": \"Here's the result of running `cat -n` on /hada/metabbo/meta_learning.py:\n     1\t\"\"\"Meta-learning based ParamController for Differential Evolution.\n     2\t\n     3\tProvides `DQNController` and `DiscreteDQNController` implementing the `ParamController` interface.\n     4\tThe controller wraps a small PyTorch network that maps observations to\n     5\tDE parameter values (F, CR, strategy). It can load/save a model\n     6\tand be used at inference time inside the DE optimizer.\n     7\t\"\"\"\n     8\tfrom typing import Dict, Any\n     9\timport os\n    10\timport numpy as np\n    11\ttry:\n    12\t    import torch\n    13\t    import torch.nn as nn\n    14\t    import torch.nn.functional as F\n    15\texcept Exception:\n    16\t    torch = None\n    17\t\n    18\tfrom param_controller import ParamController\n    19\t\n    20\timport sys\n    21\tsys.path.insert(0, os.path.dirname(os.path.dirname(os.path.abspath(__file__))))\n    22\tfrom config import SEED, set_global_seed\n    23\t\n    24\tif torch is not None:\n    25\t    class _Net(nn.Module):\n    26\t        def __init__(self, obs_dim, action_dim, seed=None):\n    27\t            super().__init__()\n    28\t            self.fc1 = nn.Linear(obs_dim, 128)\n    29\t            self.fc2 = nn.Linear(128, 128)\n    30\t            self.fc3 = nn.Linear(128, 128)\n    31\t            self.out = nn.Linear(128, action_dim)\n    32\t            \n    33\t            # \u5982\u679c\u63d0\u4f9b\u4e86\u79cd\u5b50\uff0c\u4f7f\u7528\u5b83\u6765\u521d\u59cb\u5316\u6743\u91cd\n    34\t            if seed is not None:\n    35\t                self._init_weights(seed)\n    36\t\n    37\t        def _init_weights(self, seed):\n    38\t            \"\"\"\u4f7f\u7528\u56fa\u5b9a\u79cd\u5b50\u521d\u59cb\u5316\u7f51\u7edc\u6743\u91cd\"\"\"\n    39\t            g = torch.Generator()\n    40\t            g.manual_seed(seed)\n    41\t            \n    42\t            # \u4f7f\u7528Kaiming\u521d\u59cb\u5316\n    43\t            for m in [self.fc1, self.fc2, self.fc3, self.out]:\n    44\t                torch.nn.init.kaiming_uniform_(m.weight, nonlinearity='leaky_relu')\n    45\t                torch.nn.init.zeros_(m.bias)\n    46\t\n    47\t        def forward(self, x):\n    48\t            x = x.type_as(self.fc1.weight)\n    49\t            x = F.leaky_relu(self.fc1(x), 0.2)\n    50\t            x = F.leaky_relu(self.fc2(x), 0.2)\n    51\t            x = F.leaky_relu(self.fc3(x), 0.2)\n    52\t            return self.out(x)\n    53\t\n    54\t\n    55\tclass DQNController(ParamController):\n    56\t    \"\"\"Simple continuous-action DQN controller for DE parameters.\n    57\t\n    58\t    The network outputs unbounded real values; `step` maps them to\n    59\t    DE parameter ranges: F in [0.1, 1.0], CR in [0.0, 1.0].\n    60\t    \"\"\"\n    61\t    def __init__(self, model_path: str = None, obs_dim: int = 3, device: str = 'cpu'):\n    62\t        if torch is None:\n    63\t            raise RuntimeError('torch is required for DQNController')\n    64\t        self.device = torch.device(device)\n    65\t        self.obs_dim = obs_dim\n    66\t        self.action_dim = 2  # F, CR\n    67\t        self.net = _Net(self.obs_dim, self.action_dim).to(self.device)\n    68\t        if model_path and os.path.exists(model_path):\n    69\t            self.load(model_path)\n    70\t\n    71\t    def reset(self, problem_meta: Dict[str, Any]) -> None:\n    72\t        return None\n    73\t\n    74\t    def step(self, observation: Dict[str, Any]) -> Dict[str, float]:\n    75\t        it = float(observation.get('iteration', 0))\n    76\t        evals = float(observation.get('evals', 0))\n    77\t        gbest_f = float(observation.get('gbest_f', 0.0))\n    78\t        init_f = float(observation.get('initial_gbest_f', gbest_f))\n    79\t        max_evals = float(observation.get('max_evals', max(1.0, evals)))\n    80\t        # normalized progress and relative improvement\n    81\t        progress = min(1.0, evals / max(1.0, max_evals))\n    82\t        if init_f != 0.0 and gbest_f != 0.0 and np.isfinite(init_f) and np.isfinite(gbest_f):\n    83\t            rel_improve = max(0.0, min(1.0, (np.log(abs(init_f)) - np.log(abs(gbest_f))) / max(1.0, abs(np.log(abs(init_f))))))\n    84\t        else:\n    85\t            rel_improve = 0.0\n    86\t        obs = [progress, rel_improve, gbest_f]\n    87\t        \n    88\t        net_dtype = self.net.fc1.weight.dtype\n    89\t        x = torch.tensor(obs, dtype=net_dtype, device=self.device).unsqueeze(0)\n    90\t        \n    91\t        with torch.no_grad():\n    92\t            out_t = self.net(x).squeeze(0).cpu()\n    93\t            try:\n    94\t                out_t = torch.nan_to_num(out_t, nan=0.0, posinf=1e6, neginf=-1e6)\n    95\t            except Exception:\n    96\t                out_np = out_t.numpy()\n    97\t                out_np = np.nan_to_num(out_np, nan=0.0, posinf=1e6, neginf=-1e6)\n    98\t                out_t = torch.from_numpy(out_np)\n    99\t            out_t = torch.clamp(out_t, -10.0, 10.0)\n   100\t\n   101\t        out = out_t.numpy()\n   102\t\n   103\t        # Map F (differential weight) with sigmoid to [0.1, 1.0]\n   104\t        try:\n   105\t            f_raw = float(out[0])\n   106\t            f_sig = 1.0 / (1.0 + float(np.exp(-f_raw)))\n   107\t        except Exception:\n   108\t            f_sig = 0.5\n   109\t        F = 0.1 + (1.0 - 0.1) * f_sig\n   110\t        \n   111\t        # Map CR (crossover probability) with sigmoid to [0.0, 1.0]\n   112\t        try:\n   113\t            cr_raw = float(out[1])\n   114\t            cr_sig = 1.0 / (1.0 + float(np.exp(-cr_raw)))\n   115\t        except Exception:\n   116\t            cr_sig = 0.9\n   117\t        CR = 0.0 + (1.0 - 0.0) * cr_sig\n   118\t        \n   119\t        return {'F': float(F), 'CR': float(CR)}\n   120\t\n   121\t    def save(self, path: str):\n   122\t        torch.save(self.net.state_dict(), path)\n   123\t\n   124\t    def load(self, path: str):\n   125\t        self.net.load_state_dict(torch.load(path, map_location=self.device))\n   126\t\n   127\t\n   128\tclass DiscreteDQNController(ParamController):\n   129\t    \"\"\"Discrete-action DQN controller for DE parameters with online training.\n   130\t\n   131\t    Discretizes F and CR into `bins` equally spaced values and\n   132\t    treats the Cartesian product as the discrete action space.\n   133\t    The network outpu\n   134\t<response clipped>\n   135\trm]\n   136\t        net_dtype = self.net.fc1.weight.dtype\n   137\t        return torch.tensor(obs, dtype=net_dtype, device=self.device)\n   138\t\n   139\t    def step(self, observation: Dict[str, Any]) -> Dict[str, float]:\n   140\t        x = self._obs_to_tensor(observation)\n   141\t\n   142\t        # Epsilon-greedy action selection (only in training mode)\n   143\t        if self.training and self.rng.random() < self.epsilon:\n   144\t            idx = int(self.rng.randint(self.n_actions))\n   145\t        else:\n   146\t            with torch.no_grad():\n   147\t                q = self.net(x.unsqueeze(0)).squeeze(0).cpu().numpy()\n   148\t            idx = int(np.argmax(q))\n   149\t\n   150\t        if self.training:\n   151\t            # Store transition placeholder; reward will be set later via store_reward()\n   152\t            self._last_obs = observation\n   153\t            self._last_action_idx = idx\n   154\t            self._last_obs_tensor = x\n   155\t\n   156\t        return dict(self.actions[idx])\n   157\t\n   158\t    def store_reward(self, reward: float, next_observation: Dict[str, Any], done: bool):\n   159\t        \"\"\"Store a complete transition (obs, action, reward, next_obs, done) in the replay buffer.\"\"\"\n   160\t        # Always track total reward (for both train and test)\n   161\t        self.total_reward += reward\n   162\t        self.current_sweep_reward += reward\n   163\t        \n   164\t        if not self.training:\n   165\t            return\n   166\t\n   167\t        next_obs_tensor = self._obs_to_tensor(next_observation)\n   168\t        transition = (\n   169\t            self._last_obs_tensor.clone(),\n   170\t            self._last_action_idx,\n   171\t            reward,\n   172\t            next_obs_tensor.clone(),\n   173\t            done\n   174\t        )\n   175\t\n   176\t        if len(self.replay_buffer) < self.buffer_capacity:\n   177\t            self.replay_buffer.append(transition)\n   178\t        else:\n   179\t            self.replay_buffer[self.buffer_pos] = transition\n   180\t            self.buffer_pos = (self.buffer_pos + 1) % self.buffer_capacity\n   181\t\n   182\t        self.step_count += 1\n   183\t\n   184\t        # Record return every N updates and reset counter\n   185\t        if self.step_count % self.return_record_interval == 0:\n   186\t            self.reward_history.append(self.current_sweep_reward)\n   187\t            self.current_sweep_reward = 0.0\n   188\t\n   189\t        # t) + self.f_end * t\n        CR = self.cr_start * (1 - t) + self.cr_end * t\n\n        # Detect stagnation: no improvement in best fitness for several iterations\n        current_gbest = observation.get('gbest_f', None)\n        if current_gbest is not None and self.last_gbest is not None:\n            if current_gbest < self.last_gbest - log_eps_start)\n        epsilon = float(np.exp(log_eps))\n\n        # If the current best is still infeasible late in the run, relax epsilon\n        # slightly so the algorithm is not forced into a tiny feasible region.\n        current_gbest_cv = observation.get('gbest_cv', None)\n        if current_gbest_cv is not None and t > 0.5 and current_gbest_cv > epsilon:\n            epsilon = max(epsilon, min(current_gbest_cv * 0.5, self.epsilon_start))\n\n        return {'F': float(F), 'CR': float(CR), 'strategy': strategy,\n                'epsilon': float(epsilon)}\"\n    }\n    </json>"

    "content": "<json>\n    {\n        \"tool_name\": \"editor\",\n        \"tool_input\": {\n            \"command\": \"str_replace\",\n            \"path\": \"/hada/metabbo/param_controller.py\",\n            \"old_str\": \"        return {'F': float(F), 'CR': float(CR), 'strategy': strategy,\\n                'epsilon': float(epsilon)}\",\n            \"new_str\": \"        return {'F': float(F), 'CR': float(CR), 'strategy': strategy,\\n                'epsilon': float(epsilon)}\\n\\n\\nclass LSHADEController(ParamController):\\n    \\\"\\\"\\\"Progress-aware controller for L-SHADE style algorithms.\\n\\n    Uses cosine interpolation for smooth exploration-to-exploitation,\\n    exposes `pbest_p` for controlling pbest selection pressure, and\\n    boosts F/CR on stagnation to escape local optima.\\n    \\\"\\\"\\\"\\n    def __init__(self, f_start=0.9, f_end=0.3, cr_start=0.9, cr_end=0.3,\\n                 stagnation_boost=0.2, pbest_start=0.2, pbest_end=0.05):\\n        self.f_start = f_start\\n        self.f_end = f_end\\n        self.cr_start = cr_start\\n        self.cr_end = cr_end\\n        self.stagnation_boost = stagnation_boost\\n        self.pbest_start = pbest_start\\n        self.pbest_end = pbest_end\\n\\n    def reset(self, problem_meta):\\n        self.it = 0\\n        self.last_gbest = None\\n        self.stagnation_count = 0\\n\\n    def step(self, observation):\\n        self.it = observation.get('iteration', self.it + 1)\\n        max_evals = float(observation.get('max_evals', 10000))\\n        evals = float(observation.get('evals', 0))\\n        progress = min(1.0, evals / max(1.0, max_evals))\\n\\n        # Cosine interpolation for smoother early exploration\\n        t_cos = (1.0 - np.cos(progress * np.pi)) / 2.0\\n        F = self.f_start * (1.0 - t_cos) + self.f_end * t_cos\\n        CR = self.cr_start * (1.0 - t_cos) + self.cr_end * t_cos\\n        pbest_p = self.pbest_start * (1.0 - progress) + self.pbest_end * progress\\n\\n        current_gbest = observation.get('gbest_f', None)\\n        if current_gbest is not None and self.last_gbest is not None:\\n            if current_gbest < self.last_gbest - 1e-12:\\n                self.stagnation_count = 0\\n            else:\\n                self.stagnation_count += 1\\n        self.last_gbest = current_gbest\\n\\n        if self.stagnation_count > 8:\\n            F = min(1.0, F + self.stagnation_boost)\\n            CR = min(1.0, CR + self.stagnation_boost)\\n            pbest_p = min(0.5, pbest_p + 0.1)\\n            self.stagnation_count = 0\\n\\n        return {'F': float(F), 'CR': float(CR), 'pbest_p': float(pbest_p),\\n                'strategy': 'l-shade'}\"\n        }\n    </json>",
    "text": "<json>\n    {\n        \"tool_name\": \"editor\",\n        \"tool_input\": {\n            \"command\": \"str_replace\",\n            \"path\": \"/hada/metabbo/param_controller.py\",\n            \"old_str\": \"        return {'F': float(F), 'CR': float(CR), 'strategy': strategy,\\n                'epsilon': float(epsilon)}\",\n            \"new_str\": \"        return {'F': float(F), 'CR': float(CR), 'strategy': strategy,\\n                'epsilon': float(epsilon)}\\n\\n\\nclass LSHADEController(ParamController):\\n    \\\"\\\"\\\"Progress-aware controller for L-SHADE style algorithms.\\n\\n    Uses cosine interpolation for smooth exploration-to-exploitation,\\n    exposes `pbest_p` for controlling pbest selection pressure, and\\n    boosts F/CR on stagnation to escape local optima.\\n    \\\"\\\"\\\"\\n    def __init__(self, f_start=0.9, f_end=0.3, cr_start=0.9, cr_end=0.3,\\n                 stagnation_boost=0.2, pbest_start=0.2, pbest_end=0.05):\\n        self.f_start = f_start\\n        self.f_end = f_end\\n        self.cr_start = cr_start\\n        self.cr_end = cr_end\\n        self.stagnation_boost = stagnation_boost\\n        self.pbest_start = pbest_start\\n        self.pbest_end = pbest_end\\n\\n    def reset(self, problem_meta):\\n        self.it = 0\\n        self.last_gbest = None\\n        self.stagnation_count = 0\\n\\n    def step(self, observation):\\n        self.it = observation.get('iteration', self.it + 1)\\n        max_evals = float(observation.get('max_evals', 10000))\\n        evals = float(observation.get('evals', 0))\\n        progress = min(1.0, evals / max(1.0, max_evals))\\n\\n        # Cosine interpolation for smoother early exploration\\n        t_cos = (1.0 - np.cos(progress * np.pi)) / 2.0\\n        F = self.f_start * (1.0 - t_cos) + self.f_end * t_cos\\n        CR = self.cr_start * (1.0 - t_cos) + self.cr_end * t_cos\\n        pbest_p = self.pbest_start * (1.0 - progress) + self.pbest_end * progress\\n\\n        current_gbest = observation.get('gbest_f', None)\\n        if current_gbest is not None and self.last_gbest is not None:\\n            if current_gbest < self.last_gbest - 1e-12:\\n                self.stagnation_count = 0\\n            else:\\n                self.stagnation_count += 1\\n        self.last_gbest = current_gbest\\n\\n        if self.stagnation_count > 8:\\n            F = min(1.0, F + self.stagnation_boost)\\n            CR = min(1.0, CR + self.stagnation_boost)\\n            pbest_p = min(0.5, pbest_p + 0.1)\\n            self.stagnation_count = 0\\n\\n        return {'F': float(F), 'CR': float(CR), 'pbest_p': float(pbest_p),\\n                'strategy': 'l-shade'}\"\n        }\n    </json>"
